# Native code that is built and tested and that the running app never reaches

Pinned to commit `81efb75f75b218d4f551718d4de5fd3949c64136` (branch `gpui`); every file was read from an export of that commit under this folder (`head/`), never from the working tree. Scripts are in `scripts/`, intermediate data next to this file.

## Method

1. `scripts/parse.py` walks the module tree from `app-gpui/src/lib.rs` and `main.rs` (following `mod x;`, inline `mod x {}` and `#[path]`), blanks comments and string contents, and extracts every item (fn, method, struct, enum, trait, type, const, static, impl block, macro_rules, item-level macro calls such as `pattern!`). An item is test code when it or an enclosing module carries `#[cfg(test)]`/`#[cfg(all(test, …))]`, `#[test]` or `#[gpui::test]`; that covers `mod tests`, the `tests/` folders and the `proofs*` files, which are all declared under `#[cfg(test)]`. Files that no `mod` declares are not compiled and are reported apart.
2. `scripts/graph.py` builds a name graph: an item points at every non-test item whose name it uses (a qualified use `a::b::name` is narrowed to items in a module or type named `b`, falling back to all same-named items when nothing matches); inline format arguments (`{name}`) and serde attribute paths count as uses. Reachability starts at `main` and `init` and runs to a fixpoint: free items and types are reached when a reached item names them; a method or associated const when its `Self` type is reached and its name is named somewhere reached; every method of an impl of a trait from outside the crate (Render, EventEmitter, Default, From, Display…) when its type is reached; impls of crate traits when the type and the trait are reached. `self_check` and `inspect` are left out of the first pass and added in a second, so code reached only through those harnesses is separated. A strict variant (`graph.py strict`) counts a bare lowercase name only when it is called or passed as a value and drops the qualified-name fallback.
3. `scripts/groups.py` groups unreached items by file and by type (methods with their type, free items as `<free>`). `scripts/tests_map.py` maps each test of `rust_tests.txt` to the production items it names, through its test-local helpers, and classifies it against its own subject module (the module its `tests`/`proofs*` module sits in): U touches only unreached items of it, M unreached and reached ones, R none unreached, W opens a workbench window (`Workbench::new`, `open_window`, `Target::…`). Small read-only accessors of reached types and the harness modules are not counted as unreached subjects. `scripts/rows.py` expands every citation of `parity.json` (full name or module prefix) and shortlists `done` rows with at least one U test, or only M tests: 146 rows.
4. Reading: the shortlisted rows and every file with 80 or more unreached lines or behind a shortlisted row were read by four helpers (one per area) against the export and the webview source, with production callers confirmed by grep with test modules stripped; I re-checked a sample by grep (set_locale, create_test_parser, formats_on_save, merge_timeline, PasteSession, enforce_tab_limit) and corrected one helper statement (the tab limit is not reached; see the prefs row).
5. Settings keys (`scripts/keys.py`) and handlers (`scripts/handlers.py`, `scripts/dispatch.py`), described in their sections.

### What the method cannot see

- It over-reaches by name: any reached item that names `select`, `open`, `show`, `new`… reaches every free function or method of that name on a reached type. So the unreached set is a lower bound. Measured cases: `editor::tabs::Tabs::enforce_tab_limit` counts as reached through an unrelated `select` in `editor_model/removal.rs`, and the helpers found `editor_model/guard.rs`/`removal.rs`, `features_b/color_preview` and `settings_model/settings_window.rs` (`start`, `settings_loaded`, `finished`) reached only through shared names. The strict pass marks only 202 more items unreached than the loose one, so name collisions on methods (which the strict pass keeps) are the main residue.
- It does not follow types: a method called on a value whose type is never named is credited to every type with that method name; calls through trait objects of crate traits count only by method name.
- Macros are not expanded (only `pattern!`, `re!`, `thread_local!`, `identified!` and `include!` are handled), `use … as` aliases are not resolved (one alias in the tree, harmless), and dead branches inside a reached function (an arm that never runs, a flag always false) are invisible: a reached function counts entirely.
- Reached means named from a chain that starts at `main`; it does not mean the call runs (for example a registered action no menu or key binds).
- The test map names the production items a test uses, not what it executes, and classifies against the test's own module only, so a test of a reached module that drives an unreached helper elsewhere shows as R (helper C found `folder_icons_follow_detected_and_marked_roots` and `resolve_log_branches` this way). The shortlist can therefore miss rows; it does not produce false lowerings, because every lowering was read.
- 27 tests of `rust_tests.txt` (`platform::git_ref_name::tests::…`, generated by a macro) have no parsed function and were not classified.

## Totals

- Compiled non-test items (impl blocks not counted): 27210; reached from `main`/`init`: 23171; unreached: 4039 items, 34244 lines in 511 files and 1223 script groups. Reached only through `--self-check` or the debug `inspect` driver: 37 items.
- Read and judged: 606 groups in 230 files: 591 unreached (28261 lines), 15 test support (636 lines), none reached. Not read (script only, under 80 unreached lines and behind no shortlisted row): 281 files, 5372 lines; listed at the end as Hypothesis.
- Not compiled at all (no `mod` declares them): 55 files, 11025 lines, all committed as drafts or `*_pending` proofs; none has a test in `rust_tests.txt`.
- Parity rows: 146 `done` rows shortlisted by script; a second pass listed the 73 `done` rows whose tests are M with no U and looked at the 15 of them where M tests are the majority or the inventory shows `[m]`: 7 lowered (their whole feature has no handler), 8 not judged (`projectPresentation`, `fs_read_dir`, `changelistMenu`, `directoryCompareActions`, `recentChanges`, `details`, `markerIcons`, `keymaps/windows`). In all 109 rows are lowered to `partial` (55 of them already shown `[m]` by the inventory) and 44 kept. Not read: the other 58 M-without-U rows, the 8 above, and the `[m]` row `unit:src/editor/lsp/semantic/go/index.test.ts` (its tests classify R); Hypothesis: some of them claim behaviour that only unreached methods carry.
- Settings keys written by native Settings pages: 372; with no reader at all: 52.
- Registered actions whose handler is a literal no-op or log-only closure: none found; 3 handlers that only log for whole features; 41 menu ids with no registration found (Hypothesis, see Handlers).

## Unreached groups, largest first

Ordered by file (the top of this list is by file, not by feature: the five GitHub review files together hold about 2,350 lines). Each line is one group as the helper judged it. Lines are those of the unreached items. Consequences are read from the source, not seen in the running app (Hypothesis unless the text says otherwise).

### `app-gpui/src/github/timeline.rs` (727 lines)

- **<free> + all types (TimelineEntry, SuggestionThread, ThreadAnchor, SimpleGroup, StateGroup, ReviewStatus, MentionQuery, ...)** (727 lines): Model of the pull request timeline editor: merging timeline events, review status texts, diff hunks of review threads, suggested-change patches, reactions and @mention completion. Consequence: No timeline editor exists: View Timeline in the details panel and opening a PR with its timeline only log 'not built' (workbench/github.rs:1005, :1366); the user cannot read the PR conversation, reply, react, apply suggestions or @mention. Tests: `github::timeline::tests`. Missing call: app-gpui/src/workbench/github.rs:1365. Webview: src/features/github/index.ts:284.

### `app-gpui/src/editor_lsp/imports_on_paste.rs` (512 lines)

- **PasteSession** (234 lines): Imports on paste: after a paste, waits for the server's fresh diagnostics, asks the import quick fixes for unresolved names in the pasted ranges, then asks / applies / ignores per Settings › Auto Import. Consequence: Pasting code with unresolved names never offers or adds imports; the Imports on paste setting has no effect. A native paste reaches EditorIntelligence only as EditOrigin::Other, which nothing routes to a paste session. Tests: `editor_lsp::imports_on_paste::tests`. ### `app-gpui/src/editor_model/guard.rs` (332 lines)

- **EditorGuard** (313 lines): The editor's open/close admission: generations, the serial queue every tab removal and close preparation runs through, the close decision for project/window, blur/switch autosave targets. Consequence: Native tab commands go straight to TabsState (TabCommand::Close → tabs.close_tab), not through this serial queue; nothing outside editor_model names EditorGuard or Removal. Hypothesis (app_models/close_guard.rs and the dirty-close path not read): what is lost is the guard's ordering rules (stale-open refusal, a queued removal rechecking dirtiness, blur/switch autosave targets), not dirty-close prompting as such. - `app-gpui/src/inspect.rs` <free> parse, start_from_file, start, run, perform, ... + Step, Log, Line (258 lines): A debug-build script runner that drives the real window from a step file (OPENIDEA_GPUI_INSPECT). main.rs:41-43 main -> inspect::start_from_file (debug builds, env OPENIDEA_GPUI_INSPECT)
- `app-gpui/src/self_check.rs` <free> run, text_widths, fonts, web_view, ... + Blank (173 lines): Checks only a real window can show (embedded OS web view, text widths, fonts), run by --self-check=<name> and driven from an integration test. main.rs:11-17 main -> self_check::run(check) when argv[1] is --self-check=NAME
- `app-gpui/src/find/popup_view.rs` FindPopup (49 lines): Inspectors the window tests read; the diff they read is produced by the reached shown_diff / replace_preview path. ## Handlers that do nothing

`scripts/handlers.py` read every `.handle("id", closure)` registration in non-test code (253 with a literal id) and found no closure whose body is empty, `Ok(())` or only `tracing` calls. `scripts/dispatch.py` looked for `match id`/`match action` blocks whose `_` arm does nothing and found only local button dispatchers (`press(id)` of views), none fed by registered action ids.

Handlers that only log (grep over production code for `tracing::` calls saying not built / not carried out): `app-gpui/src/workbench/services_window.rs:739`; `app-gpui/src/workbench/github.rs:1005`; `app-gpui/src/workbench/github.rs:1354`. They cover the Docker operations of Services and, in GitHub, View Timeline, the details flows and Merge.
